It all just kind of blurred together, I guess.\n\nDoctor: Did your feelings of sadness and depression start before, after, or around the same time you stopped wanting to eat?\n\nPatient: I... like I just said, I really don't know. Maybe around the same time? I just wasn't paying attention to which one happened first.\n\nDoctor: Has your weight loss been so significant that other people have noticed or commented on it?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I guess they have. Um... I stopped caring about tracking my food. I'd just... skip meals for days. Mostly just having coffee and protein bars. And... yeah, my clothes started hanging loose on me before I even realized it.\n\nDoctor: Do you think you would have started eating normally again if you hadn't been feeling sad and depressed?\n\nPatient: I... I'm not sure. It's hard to say what I would have done. I just... I really don't know how to answer that. I just know I didn't want to eat.\n\nDoctor: Did you ever try to eat but found you just weren't able to because you weren't hungry, or did you sometimes think about eating but chose not to?\n\nPatient: I don't really know. I just... didn't eat. I wasn't really paying attention to whether I was hungry or making some kind of choice about it. It just didn't happen.\n\nDoctor: Did you want to lose weight at any point during this time?\n\nPatient: No. I wasn't... trying to do that. It wasn't about losing weight.\n\nDoctor: Did you feel like you were making a choice not to eat, or did you simply not think about food?\n\nPatient: I... I don't think it was a choice. I didn't even realize how bad it was until my clothes started hanging off me. I just... wasn't eating real meals.\n\nDoctor: Did you feel like you were avoiding food or just not interested in eating when you weren't eating?\n\nPatient: Just not interested. I wasn't avoiding it... my eating just went completely off the rails. Like I said, I just didn't care.\n\nDoctor: Did you feel like something was preventing you from eating when you weren't eating, or was it just that you didn't want to?\n\nPatient: No, nothing was preventing me. I just... didn't want to. Like I told you, I just stopped caring about real meals and lived on coffee for days. I didn't even notice what was happening until my clothes were loose.\n\nDoctor: Is your weight loss something that worries you now?\n\nPatient: I don't know. I guess... not really. It just is what it is.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your lack of interest in eating would have happened if you weren't feeling sad and depressed?\n\nPatient: I don't really know. I guess... probably not.
